# ALL LOOT CHECK-LIST

Fonte: https://steamcommunity.com/sharedfiles/filedetails/?id=3035525626

## Introduction

**First of all, English is not my native language, so I apologize for any grammatical errors.  
  
Obviously, this guide will contain spoilers. If you want to avoid them, it's best not to use this guide during your first playthrough.**  
  
This guide is not a step-by-step walkthrough, so you won't find exact information on how to reach specific areas in the game. Instead, it serves as a list to help you identify items you might have missed, along with their coordinates in their respective areas. However, I've also included a link to the [Wiki](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2F)[bg3.wiki] for each item in case you need more details about it.  
  
**Disclaimer: The seller's price may vary depending on your charisma and attitude.**

## Prologue

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167038982421/6AED5C835AB31BC58A12188994B88D7A60B0DF14/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167038982421/6AED5C835AB31BC58A12188994B88D7A60B0DF14/)

[Everburn Blade](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FEverburn_Blade)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from Commander Zhalk at the helm of the Nautiloid during the Prologue or can also be obtained by casting Command (Drop) on Commander Zhalk instead of killing him, though his chance of succeeding the Saving Throw is high.

## Act 1 - Dank Crypt

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[The Watcher's Guide](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Watcher%2527s_Guide)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a trapped sarcophagus with a booby trap where traps will shoot exploding fireballs at you from either side if you do not disable them by pressing the button after opening the sarcophagus.

Looted from a heavy chest in the same room that Withers emerges.

[Gloves of Power](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FGloves_of_Power)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from the Goblin Boss Za'Krug in front of the Druid Grove.

[Silver Pendant](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSilver_Pendant)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from a skeleton on the cliff southwest of the Druid Grove by jumping up two small ridges and climbing a wooden ladder to reach this area. **(X:160, Y:373)**

Given by Tara during the first Long Rest following completion of the Escape the Nautiloid quest (only when playing as the Gale Origin).

Auntie Ethel

  
She is a fussy trader of potions, lotions, quirks, and sundries who can be first encountered in The Hollow of the Emerald Grove. Later, she will return to her home at the Riverside Teahouse in the Sunlit Wetlands.  
  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

## Druid Grove

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039161731/367329299F92D686902B9C6B6531617855543CFB/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039161731/367329299F92D686902B9C6B6531617855543CFB/)

[Shapeshifter's Boon Ring](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FShapeshifter%2527s_Boon_Ring)[bg3.wiki]

Can be looted from the Strange Ox in the Druid Grove, or later at Dammon's blacksmith in the Last Light Inn. (If you kill the Strange Ox, you won't be able to complete the "Help the Devilish Ox" quest.)

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039177158/6A2E64167D41FE0CC27E06D8A16CE2ED4618FC47/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039177158/6A2E64167D41FE0CC27E06D8A16CE2ED4618FC47/)

[The Whispering Promise](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Whispering_Promise)[bg3.wiki]

Sold by Volo in the Druid Grove, who may not initially appear to be a trader, but there is a coin icon button in the bottom left that will allow you to engage in trading with him, and also sold by Grat the Trader in the Goblin Camp.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261080186/BDDB9B475AC535C4628FE4C51643868DC54B4767/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261080186/BDDB9B475AC535C4628FE4C51643868DC54B4767/)

[Instrument Proficiency](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FProficiency%23Instrument_Proficiency)[bg3.wiki]

Pass two Charisma (Performance) checks while helping Alfira with her song in the Druid Grove.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039198843/201F04745882B0D59CAF9BACC3F49C152F5FC9B7/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039198843/201F04745882B0D59CAF9BACC3F49C152F5FC9B7/)

[Cap of Curing](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCap_of_Curing)[bg3.wiki]

This hat is found in a locked gilded chest behind a pillar near to where Alfira is composing her song in the Druid Grove **(X:289, Y:488).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039210416/B7A6836011ADC6B3295C181BB255CA370A4D70A7/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039210416/B7A6836011ADC6B3295C181BB255CA370A4D70A7/)

[Ring of Colour Spray](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRing_of_Colour_Spray)[bg3.wiki]

Can be found in the Harpys' Nest in the Secluded Cove **(X:326, Y:495).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039221740/D3A675D9BC2591163CDB6C67D242E1E794D222FF/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039221740/D3A675D9BC2591163CDB6C67D242E1E794D222FF/)

[Amulet of Silvanus](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAmulet_of_Silvanus)[bg3.wiki]

Hidden under a rock that must be moved, near Ormn the bear in the Druid Grove **(X:262, Y:481).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039233918/4075E043D5FAACB4B3B789AFD3C3D2FB94BFCA49/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039233918/4075E043D5FAACB4B3B789AFD3C3D2FB94BFCA49/)

[Nature's Snare](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FNature%2527s_Snare)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from a locked chest within the Underground Passage of the Druid Grove, with the key to the chest located on a corpse on the lower part of the island in the middle, guarded by two turret statues. **(X:-425, Y:-200)**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039245796/B2AF76E2450B7FF2ED680DE576DC637B239A05DE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039245796/B2AF76E2450B7FF2ED680DE576DC637B239A05DE/)

[Key of the Ancients](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FKey_of_the_Ancients)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Findal in the Underground Passage connecting to the Druid Grove and also by Nettie at Druid Grove.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039255619/32477677617863825971443E18AD3D76BDEC47A0/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039255619/32477677617863825971443E18AD3D76BDEC47A0/)

[Komira's Locket](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FKomira%2527s_Locket)[bg3.wiki]

Given to you by Arabella's parents after protecting her from Kagha in "Save Arabella".

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039272022/AE54BD537138B6DC8550A4A90E4DA43F248A54C1/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039272022/AE54BD537138B6DC8550A4A90E4DA43F248A54C1/)

[Broodmother's Revenge](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBroodmother%2527s_Revenge)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Kagha at Druid Grove.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039277698/1226D77607682C3CE239DF75F1E1F93D4CF3A980/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039277698/1226D77607682C3CE239DF75F1E1F93D4CF3A980/)

[Hellrider's Pride](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHellrider%2527s_Pride)[bg3.wiki]

Carried by Zevlor in the Druid Grove and can be either bought from him, stolen or earned as a reward for "Investigate Kagha".

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039282672/4D06E3675281D87956D6FF003A29D058C81B7312/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039282672/4D06E3675281D87956D6FF003A29D058C81B7312/)

[Sorrow](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSorrow)[bg3.wiki]

Found in the Hidden Vault within the Druid Grove. You will need the Rune of the Wolf carried by Rath to access the vault; which you can obtain by talking to Rath after rescuing Halsin, pickpocketing him, or killing him.

  

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039303583/73FFC13913BE94067E15DC336AB05F1A1D6B5BC6/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039303583/73FFC13913BE94067E15DC336AB05F1A1D6B5BC6/)

[Robe of Summer](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRobe_of_Summer)[bg3.wiki]

Found inside a crate sitting in the Hidden Vault within the Druid Grove. You will need the Rune of the Wolf carried by Rath to access the vault; which you can obtain by talking to Rath after rescuing Halsin, pickpocketing him, or killing him.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039130514/EFE0F78DCE4A26DA101D76973FE012262F58CB0B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039130514/EFE0F78DCE4A26DA101D76973FE012262F58CB0B/)

[Pale Oak](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FPale_Oak)[bg3.wiki]

Obtained as a reward for completing the quest "Investigate Kagha" and both persuading and sparing Kagha, which must be completed before the quest "Rescue the Druid Halsin". In this scenario, Kagha will give you the Pale Oak. The Pale Oak staff is only awarded if you are a druid.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487758404/99920D3E58E830E31FFF4A8661909EFF5DDB6C24/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487758404/99920D3E58E830E31FFF4A8661909EFF5DDB6C24/)

[Ring of Protection](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRing_of_Protection)[bg3.wiki]

Rewarded by Mol for completing the quest "Steal the Sacred Idol".

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263749122/37117EF9A5FBD4572DEC87B5ED0F5D568B2B9AAF/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263749122/37117EF9A5FBD4572DEC87B5ED0F5D568B2B9AAF/)

[The Deathstalker Mantle](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Deathstalker_Mantle)[bg3.wiki]

Given to a player using The Dark Urge Origin by Sceleritas Fel as a reward for murdering the Bard.

## Owlbear Nest

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039100814/EC0FC9C2D9B445123D47C0F07D8C53CD1E409974/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039100814/EC0FC9C2D9B445123D47C0F07D8C53CD1E409974/)

[The Oakfather's Embrace](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Oakfather%2527s_Embrace)[bg3.wiki]

Lootable from a headless skeleton, on Owlbear Nest.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039355220/E05C3DA1DE5AC0B7F20B01CB7BBC7510466473C1/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039355220/E05C3DA1DE5AC0B7F20B01CB7BBC7510466473C1/)

[Moondrop Pendant](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMoondrop_Pendant)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a Selûnite Glided Chest in the Owlbear Nest. You must read the Selûnite Prayer Sheet in front of the chest to unlock it. The sheet can be spotted on the ground behind the statue of Selûne by passing a Perception check.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039444621/19FFA077CB94ED8EAA60A6BEA70118854E2683EE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039444621/19FFA077CB94ED8EAA60A6BEA70118854E2683EE/)

[Vision of the Absolute](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FVision_of_the_Absolute)[bg3.wiki]

Repaired by finding and combining the Shaft of a Broken Spear and the Head of a Broken Spear (by right-clicking on one of the items in your inventory and pressing 'combine', then dragging the other item into the open slot.) Shaft of a Broken Spear: Found on the body of Edowin in the forest west of the Druid Grove **(X: 136, Y: 446)**, and Head of a Broken Spear: Looted from the Owlbear in the Owlbear Nest.

  

Outside Owlbear Nest

  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[Find Familiar: Scratch](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FScratch)[bg3.wiki]

Leave Scratch with your scent so he joins your camp, then later when he offers you a ball, throw it to play fetch with him.

## Sunlit Wetlands

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039459840/1FF1CDCB7FEF31EBECBF152412D934C2EA0745B6/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039459840/1FF1CDCB7FEF31EBECBF152412D934C2EA0745B6/)

[The Sparkle Hands](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Sparkle_Hands)[bg3.wiki]

On the island across the river, where a group of Mud Mephits are located near a tree, you'll find a chest positioned with its back facing the mainland. **(X:95, Y:210).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039262368/EBEEDEBEC56136FEACA3B04F3793DB6DC5B5F1D2/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039262368/EBEEDEBEC56136FEACA3B04F3793DB6DC5B5F1D2/)

[Wood Woad Shield](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FWood_Woad_Shield)[bg3.wiki]

Can be looted from Wood Woads in the Sunlit Wetlands.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039467657/BA65FE503EC1F840671D2165355BB2B113948890/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039467657/BA65FE503EC1F840671D2165355BB2B113948890/)

[Gandrel's Aspiration](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FGandrel%2527s_Aspiration)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Gandrel, he is a monster hunter, who can be found just to the Southwest of the Riverside Teahouse waypoint.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039477947/8D0FAF1AA7E36A890FC41CD19A0B90EE7B49CB32/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039477947/8D0FAF1AA7E36A890FC41CD19A0B90EE7B49CB32/)

[Tarnished Charm](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FTarnished_Charm)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from Auntie Ethel in the Riverside Teahouse.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039480961/D0C743467367BEF4F25AF6CEC8ADAD0F7CB1366E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039480961/D0C743467367BEF4F25AF6CEC8ADAD0F7CB1366E/)

[The Ever-Seeing Eye](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Ever-Seeing_Eye)[bg3.wiki]

Located in the treasure room in the Hag's Lair underneath the Gnarled Teahouse.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039485581/0584C558ED3DF1725BD058849D6FF4D2A0614D23/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039485581/0584C558ED3DF1725BD058849D6FF4D2A0614D23/)

[Staff of Crones](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FStaff_of_Crones)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from the ground in the treasure room of the Ancient Abode (the Hag's lair) beneath Auntie Ethel's Teahouse.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487748017/20F22B5B6623A1E999893E9D2A7391A8BE962C52/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487748017/20F22B5B6623A1E999893E9D2A7391A8BE962C52/)

[Auntie Ethel's Hair](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAuntie_Ethel%2527s_Hair)[bg3.wiki]

After Auntie Ethel is weakened (below 20% Hit Points) and has a turn to act, she begs for her life and implores you to let her keep Mayrina and her baby, offering you power in return. The player has the possibility to intimidate or deceive Auntie Ethel to free Mayrina and get this power both, at the cost of letting Auntie Ethel go.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261031405/5342726E828983D1E3658EA6467EB22F01498600/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261031405/5342726E828983D1E3658EA6467EB22F01498600/)

[Paid the Price](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FPaid_the_Price_%28Condition%29)[bg3.wiki]

Consider Auntie Ethel's offer to eliminate the parasite at the Riverside Teahouse, but keep in mind it's mutually exclusive with Volo's Ersatz Eye and Wyll's stone eye options; you can only undergo one questionable eye surgery per character.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263606839/EF3C7F1E59B9E4D414F82253CFFC64B37F527F0B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263606839/EF3C7F1E59B9E4D414F82253CFFC64B37F527F0B/)

[Mayrina's Locket](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMayrina%2527s_Locket)[bg3.wiki]

Mayrina's Locket can be obtained by either looting it from her corpse or persuading her to give it to you after learning Auntie Ethel's intentions through Speak with Dead.

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[Warped Headband of Intellect](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FWarped_Headband_of_Intellect)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Lump the Enlightened in the Blighted Village **(X:13, Y:366)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039524879/23EC8C766C75EF680C6D33CFDD7604E1F28A6991/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039524879/23EC8C766C75EF680C6D33CFDD7604E1F28A6991/)

[Haste Helm](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHaste_Helm)[bg3.wiki]

Found inside the locked chest near to the Ancient Rune Circle in the Blighted Village **(X:32, Y:403)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039529013/37719BD94DFE501785312751A1F8265BBEB8DAFB/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039529013/37719BD94DFE501785312751A1F8265BBEB8DAFB/)

[Very Heavy Greataxe](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FVery_Heavy_Greataxe)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Fezzerk in front of the windmill in the Blighted Village.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263311473/6ADB82C3DE369EF3B3F96EC97F7902BFB662B661/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263311473/6ADB82C3DE369EF3B3F96EC97F7902BFB662B661/)

[Bloody Amulet](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBloody_Amulet)[bg3.wiki]

Can be obtain from Barcus Wroot's corpse, located at different places: tied to the windmill in the Blighted Village **(X:0, Y:425)**, in Grymforge if you freed him, or in your Camp if you recruited him in Grymforge.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039532643/EEF5844B7B57F911683A2339CE7B5A969E8D8983/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039532643/EEF5844B7B57F911683A2339CE7B5A969E8D8983/)

[The Speedy Lightfeet](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Speedy_Lightfeet)[bg3.wiki]

Found inside a Heavy Chest in the cellar behind the windmill in the Blighted Village.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039610290/B2CBCE6FA746844DE3670063D70F11D5963DCCAE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039610290/B2CBCE6FA746844DE3670063D70F11D5963DCCAE/)

[Bracers of Defence](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBracers_of_Defence)[bg3.wiki]

In the secret area of the Apothecary's Cellar beneath the Blighted Village. The bracers are located in a gilded chest **(X: -656 Y: -370)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039567077/A054544846C228CD2C1A0152C23B4FD4E8A13AA1/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039567077/A054544846C228CD2C1A0152C23B4FD4E8A13AA1/)

[Steelforged Sword](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSteelforged_Sword)[bg3.wiki]

This weapon can be found in a locked chest in the abandoned workshop in the Blighted Village, across from the Ancient Rune Circle **(X:45, Y:408)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039587656/108715E2D548C155495BE3AF33D49D357AF2565F/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039587656/108715E2D548C155495BE3AF33D49D357AF2565F/)

[Sussur Weapons](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FFinish_the_Masterwork_Weapon)[bg3.wiki]

To forge a weapon, first, light the furnace, then use the nearby bellows, combine Sussur bark with the furnace, and finally, use a plain dagger, sickle, or greatsword in the active furnace to create a Sussur weapon with a +1 enchantment and the ability to silence on hit. Sussur Tree Bark can be obtained from the Sussur Tree in the Dread Hollow in the Underdark **(X: -14, Y: -138)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487754641/AB784EE82894B8A973E98CE32C4F39035F37F0CE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487754641/AB784EE82894B8A973E98CE32C4F39035F37F0CE/)

[Summon Scroll: Cheeky Quasit](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FScroll_of_Summon_Quasit)[bg3.wiki]

In the Apothecary's Cellar, behind the moveable bookshelf, in the casket immediately to your right as you enter the cavernous room.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261101055/F7A5BCCF2A7A597EA9C243301123ACE935FEA70F/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261101055/F7A5BCCF2A7A597EA9C243301123ACE935FEA70F/)

[Necromancy of Thay](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FNecromancy_of_Thay)[bg3.wiki]

Turn all three pages while reading Necromancy of Thay, a book found in a hidden area in the Apothecary's Cellar beneath the Blighted Village. Turning only 1 page will grant only Speak with Dead. The reader does not need to succeed on the Saving Throws associated with turning the pages.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261385116/0FDAA76BA5FE82B29B20A1A29E3DB8AECD3F18B9/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261385116/0FDAA76BA5FE82B29B20A1A29E3DB8AECD3F18B9/)

[Infernal Iron](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FInfernal_Iron)[bg3.wiki]

Inside a chest in the basement of a house **(X: -434 Y: -370)**.

Found inside a wooden chest at the back of the hidden room in the cellar of the Toll House **(X: -674 Y: -401)**. The room can be opened by sitting on the two thrones simultaneously (or by placing objects on each throne).

## Waukeen's Rest

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261456084/FD67A4A4EFF2403D0CD294FC238A1DA84D098A8B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261456084/FD67A4A4EFF2403D0CD294FC238A1DA84D098A8B/)

[Svartlebee's Woundseeker](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSvartlebee%2527s_Woundseeker)[bg3.wiki]

Carried by Gauntlet Yeva.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167040104722/F3F3EE609FA097FBB76BDC65A7851271E392B1FF/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167040104722/F3F3EE609FA097FBB76BDC65A7851271E392B1FF/)

[Hamarhraft](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHamarhraft)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a Gilded Chest in one of the burning rooms on the second floor of the inn at Waukeen's Rest **(X: -84, Y:630)**. This chest can be difficult to get to before the room becomes completely engulfed in flames, so it's recommended to approach in Turn-Based Mode. The weapon needs to be picked up before the first long rest after coming to Waukeen's Rest for the first time. If you come to the burning room after a long rest, the weapon will be unavailable.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167040088181/618F88AF53E53642C868DAF4142D39CD9F2757C2/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167040088181/618F88AF53E53642C868DAF4142D39CD9F2757C2/)

[Counsellor Florrick's Weapons](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRescue_the_Grand_Duke)[bg3.wiki]

Reward for rescuing Counsellor Florrick from the burning inn of Waukeen's Rest during the quest "Rescue the Grand Duke", where Counsellor Florrick offers the party a choice between three items with the shared ability names: [The Sparky Points](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Sparky_Points)[bg3.wiki], [The Joltshooter](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Joltshooter)[bg3.wiki], and [The Spellsparkler](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Spellsparkler)[bg3.wiki]

## Zhentarim Hideout

Brem

  
Brem is a Zhentarim-aligned trader found in the Zhentarim Basement. Rescuing the Risen Road duo and their cargo earns Zarys' favor, granting access to Brem's magical items and Harold. Alternatively, save them and persuade/intimidate letting you have the cargo **(X: 300 Y: -247)**.

## Underdark

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041793993/9B009056EB58F4D0287EE0694CB677F8A05E347E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041793993/9B009056EB58F4D0287EE0694CB677F8A05E347E/)

[Helmet of Smiting](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHelmet_of_Smiting)[bg3.wiki]

This helmet can be found inside a locked gilded chest in the Selûnite Outpost **(X:163, Y:-248)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263584685/A17132BBD29E1CA521EB4921D1A9FC47276407E8/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263584685/A17132BBD29E1CA521EB4921D1A9FC47276407E8/)

[Luminous Armour](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FLuminous_Armour)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a locked and trapped Opulent Chest in the Selûnite Outpost area of the Underdark **(X: 176 Y: -247)**. The chest is behind a hidden door revealed by a Perception check, or it can be accessed by jumping from the room with the waypoint.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041787713/59AFF19EE29F53CFDBBE5A89F84246CE1D897C6B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041787713/59AFF19EE29F53CFDBBE5A89F84246CE1D897C6B/)

[Phalar Aluve](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FPhalar_Aluve)[bg3.wiki]

This weapon can be found stabbed into a rock in the Underdark at coordinates **(X:116, Y:-192)**, and can be freed by passing either a DC 15 Strength check to yank it from the stone or a DC 15 Religion check to learn how to release the magic holding it in place. Alternatively, a Cleric of Eilistraee can release the sword without needing an ability check.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041799212/83D362663AA8E0B2352F9974438CDC6F4F9A3935/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041799212/83D362663AA8E0B2352F9974438CDC6F4F9A3935/)

[The Blast Pendant](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Blast_Pendant)[bg3.wiki]

This amulet is worn by Dhourn, one of the petrified drow to the west of the Selûnite Outpost **(X:121, Y:-245).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041812802/C68A4E3DBB6CA0B90FEF5D06726CB2ABC6FFF0C1/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041812802/C68A4E3DBB6CA0B90FEF5D06726CB2ABC6FFF0C1/)

[The Spectator Eyes](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Spectator_Eyes)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from the Spectator located in the Underdark.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041820270/4DBA2824EF7DD14C5B91CF919E05BED2746D9FDE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041820270/4DBA2824EF7DD14C5B91CF919E05BED2746D9FDE/)

[Bloodguzzler Garb](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBloodguzzler_Garb)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by the Bulette in the Underdark.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041694520/AA06C9FD1F772BE8A6F249F5F1AA02DE5B9BB124/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041694520/AA06C9FD1F772BE8A6F249F5F1AA02DE5B9BB124/)

[Amulet of the Unworthy](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAmulet_of_the_Unworthy)[bg3.wiki]

Carried by a Minotaur wandering the Underdark **(X: 160 Y: -147)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041700356/04491B99D04E53C00D9A197F8EC4164FD7932008/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041700356/04491B99D04E53C00D9A197F8EC4164FD7932008/)

[Shortsword of First Blood](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FShortsword_of_First_Blood)[bg3.wiki]

Found on the corpse of an executed Deep Gnome in the Underdark at **(X:73, Y:-187)**, at the entrance of the Decrepit Village (if approaching from the Myconid Colony).

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041712566/96A04589FA45E310C1AE3A199A263EF3DE035FED/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041712566/96A04589FA45E310C1AE3A199A263EF3DE035FED/)

[Exterminator's Axe](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FExterminator%2527s_Axe)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Gekh Goal, a member of the Duergar Slave Hunters in Decrepit Village, Underdark.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487764680/600FE7290B6D5FD890E7322377208A48C29E58A5/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934043487764680/600FE7290B6D5FD890E7322377208A48C29E58A5/)

[Explorer's Ring](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FExplorer%2527s_Ring)[bg3.wiki]

Explorer's Ring can be found on a skeleton atop the stones surrounding the Bibberbang field north of the Dread Hollow region of the Underdark X: -12 Y: -50. It can be reached by jumping from a series of rock ledges or by teleporting up to the location.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261475352/831F7F466BF8D37D118D7BA44738467ED3577902/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261475352/831F7F466BF8D37D118D7BA44738467ED3577902/)

[Mourning Frost](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMourning_Frost)[bg3.wiki]

Formed by combining components carried by three Drow mages competing to discover the Adamantine Forge, found at various Underdark locations. The Icy Helve is with Dhourn west of the Selûnite Outpost, Icy Metal looted from the Pale Corpse in the Myconid Colony's secret area unlocked by completing the quest "Defeat the Duergar Intruders" **(X:50, Y:75)**, and the Icy Crystal carried by Filro the Forgotten near the Sussur Tree.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261495754/FE873A1CEA730B2781A09B20B44C32A7163BF526/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261495754/FE873A1CEA730B2781A09B20B44C32A7163BF526/)

[Dog Collar](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FDog_Collar)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a grave on the cliff overlooking Arcane Tower to the south of the Underdark - Sussur Tree Waypoint (On the 4th floor of the Arcane Tower, there's a button in the North-Eastern corner. When pressed, it seems inactive. However, if you wear the Dog Collar and interact with it, you'll discover it's actually a food dispenser for Lenore's pet, as it dispenses a piece of raw steak).

## The Festering Cove

The entrance to The Festering Cove can be found in the Torchstalk field northeast of the Arcane Tower and southeast of the Decrepit Village. There is a Cragged Rock **(X: 76 Y: -242)** that can be reached by jumping over two mushrooms growing out of the cliff face. Climb down the Cragged Rock to reach The Festering Cove.  
  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041718948/75913910C78D77213FEC9ADFF059A9B6E2B86D8B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041718948/75913910C78D77213FEC9ADFF059A9B6E2B86D8B/)

[Drow Studded Leather Armour](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FDrow_Studded_Leather_Armour)[bg3.wiki]

Inside a Heavy Chest at Lolth's Cultist's cache **(X:71 Y:-220)**, climbing up a high spot near the entrance of The Festering Cove.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041730749/3FD6A613049F91407B3BCBD430858D89109F6497/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041730749/3FD6A613049F91407B3BCBD430858D89109F6497/)

[Sickle of BOOOAL](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSickle_of_BOOOAL)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped or pickpocketed from Pooldripp the Zealous in The Festering Cove, this weapon can also be acquired by choosing the correct dialog options when speaking to BOOOAL in The Festering Cove, causing his power to falter and agreeing to become his Chosen. Alternatively, selecting different dialog options during the cutscene allows you to eliminate BOOOAL without provoking the Kuo-toa into attacking. Once this is achieved, you can then purchase the weapon from Pooldripp the Zealous (190 gp).

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041751282/CA059C7B496EB9162981686653A6E2DF8F7001D3/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041751282/CA059C7B496EB9162981686653A6E2DF8F7001D3/)

[Slippery Chain Shirt](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSlippery_Chain_Shirt)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a chest hidden on a cliff in The Festering Cove **(X:-400, Y:329).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041757381/862A1954FF430F2422E60D96F351A83C3C369524/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041757381/862A1954FF430F2422E60D96F351A83C3C369524/)

[Helmet of Autonomy](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHelmet_of_Autonomy)[bg3.wiki]

Found on a skeleton at the entrance to The Festering Cove **(X:82, Y:-241).**

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261022601/7E0D3E7AC6B0650AE0AA0924B3BE2E7DB13F545E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261022601/7E0D3E7AC6B0650AE0AA0924B3BE2E7DB13F545E/)

[BOOOAL's Benediction](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBOOOAL%2527s_Blessing_%28Condition%29)[bg3.wiki]

## Arcane Tower

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041863050/641E5DFF25EE327B1ED6991154DCE6B0D55BBCEE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041863050/641E5DFF25EE327B1ED6991154DCE6B0D55BBCEE/)

[Skybreaker](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSkybreaker)[bg3.wiki]

In a locked chest in front of the Arcane Tower in the Underdark **(X:27, Y:-298)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041879272/FB81BA109E14136B2ED8D5E47012EAEF01BE1189/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041879272/FB81BA109E14136B2ED8D5E47012EAEF01BE1189/)

[Uncovered Mysteries](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FUncovered_Mysteries)[bg3.wiki]

This amulet can be found inside a small gilded chest on the Arcane Tower's 1st floor. (Near the elevator)

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041873362/B97C88A2FC51C281A9D3FCC0ED92B0F856A68609/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041873362/B97C88A2FC51C281A9D3FCC0ED92B0F856A68609/)

[Mystra's Grace](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMystra%2527s_Grace)[bg3.wiki]

These boots can be found in the Chest of the Mundane on the northern balcony of the Arcane Tower's third floor, the same level as the arcane turrets **(X: -36 Y: 265)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041883881/A66E178EF1BCF31751A0616097F381D54A7A5938/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041883881/A66E178EF1BCF31751A0616097F381D54A7A5938/)

[Mage's Friend](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMage%2527s_Friend)[bg3.wiki]

This ring can be found in a chest at the foot of the bed on the 4th floor of the Arcane Tower.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041893294/B4942FD72D5A652B5D8A4DBFDB636FD05C6AF440/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041893294/B4942FD72D5A652B5D8A4DBFDB636FD05C6AF440/)

[Guiding Light](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FGuiding_Light)[bg3.wiki]

This ring can be obtained in the Arcane Tower by telling Bernard the appropriate play line. Bernard will place the ring on a nearby table, where the ring can be taken. Alternatively, it can be looted from Bernard after defeating it.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041897087/E70B1E5E38520FAC97433DFAF04FF286EC567ADD/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041897087/E70B1E5E38520FAC97433DFAF04FF286EC567ADD/)

[Light of Creation](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FLight_of_Creation)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Bernard on the top floor of the Arcane Tower in the Underdark.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041901232/E72869C9FEFE4CF0B9D6ECC18098D8C22FC94573/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041901232/E72869C9FEFE4CF0B9D6ECC18098D8C22FC94573/)

[Club of Hill Giant Strength](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FClub_of_Hill_Giant_Strength)[bg3.wiki]

Found by breaking the Stool of Hill Giant Strength on the highest floor of Arcane Tower, with Bernard. The stool is underneath the thatched roof section and is knocked over on its side.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041909643/DC8B9C0BF8B6A5D89597F44B393083E2C53F75D6/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041909643/DC8B9C0BF8B6A5D89597F44B393083E2C53F75D6/)

[Staff of Arcane Blessing](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FStaff_of_Arcane_Blessing)[bg3.wiki]

This staff is discovered leaning against a table in the basement of the Arcane Tower, which can be accessed by wearing the Guiding Light. The ring serves to activate a concealed button on the tower's teleporter pad, granting access to the basement. Alternatively, you can reach the Arcane Tower's basement by ascending the stairs behind the generator, utilizing Feather Fall, and leaping down into the gap between the wall and the wooden staircase.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041930429/947FBDF066329C201298B7B0EE743EDA9410B01C/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041930429/947FBDF066329C201298B7B0EE743EDA9410B01C/)

[The Sparkswall](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Sparkswall)[bg3.wiki]

This ring can be found in a gilded chest in the Arcane Tower's basement. Guiding Light is required to access the basement.

## Zurkhwood Skiffs

Primary entry to Grymforge is through the Decrepit Village by taking the skiff on the beach docks.  
  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043894284/57069CF1DA223AD81C65B9A26416D3A3627EBC32/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043894284/57069CF1DA223AD81C65B9A26416D3A3627EBC32/)

[Shining Staver-of-Skulls](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FShining_Staver-of-Skulls)[bg3.wiki]

Can be looted from or sold by Corsair Greymon, a duergar on the Ebon Lake boat to Grymforge. Greymon can be found in the main chamber, if not killed on the boat.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043897681/DF382B304256599764DAF4F898E685DB9C061F3F/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043897681/DF382B304256599764DAF4F898E685DB9C061F3F/)

[Bow of the Banshee](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBow_of_the_Banshee)[bg3.wiki]

Can be looted from or sold by Corsair Greymon. Greymon is first encountered when taking the boat found in the Decrepit Village to Grymforge. If he survives the journey, Greymon will be in Grymforge among the other Duergar.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043902055/2A19FD0890B070C5862E2322CAA19BE4BB7D0249/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167043902055/2A19FD0890B070C5862E2322CAA19BE4BB7D0249/)

[Jorgoral's Greatsword](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FJorgoral%2527s_Greatsword)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from Corsair Greymon in Ebon Lake, Grymforge.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261573038/DF78BA127E570F2454751583F5D3BFCC9DBA9A2C/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261573038/DF78BA127E570F2454751583F5D3BFCC9DBA9A2C/)

[Intransigent Warhammer](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FIntransigent_Warhammer)[bg3.wiki]

## Grymforge

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261385116/0FDAA76BA5FE82B29B20A1A29E3DB8AECD3F18B9/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261385116/0FDAA76BA5FE82B29B20A1A29E3DB8AECD3F18B9/)

Can be acquired from Stonemason Kith by passing three checks (History, Investigation, and Perception) during conversation. If these checks fail, the alloy can also be obtained by killing or purchasing it from Kith.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044266318/3593D22041077FEDA37C7049A8630C95A9738273/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044266318/3593D22041077FEDA37C7049A8630C95A9738273/)

Looted from a skeleton near the Grymforge Ancient Rune Circle in Grymforge at **(X: -655, Y: 424)**, and south of the Ancient Forge Ancient Rune Circle in Grymforge at **(X: -609, Y: 246)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044282967/7DE0B35D262AB74744704292940422A4B908E7A8/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044282967/7DE0B35D262AB74744704292940422A4B908E7A8/)

[Ring of Absolute Force](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRing_of_Absolute_Force)[bg3.wiki]

## The Adamantine Forge

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044535811/7933BB431A2482E91430AAFE4A0D3DB6B1041BAD/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044535811/7933BB431A2482E91430AAFE4A0D3DB6B1041BAD/)

[Mithral Ore](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMithral_Ore)[bg3.wiki]

Mithral Ore is an ingredient found from one of two Ore Veins in Abandoned Refuge. The first is located northeast of the Adamantine Forge, across some lava, against the cavern wall at **(X: -556, Y: 277)**. The second can be found northwest of Abandoned Refuge, also across some lava, next to a cliff at **(X: -642, Y: 253)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044384497/A7F4106D4035759E4BE93804EB623EBAF9508383/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044384497/A7F4106D4035759E4BE93804EB623EBAF9508383/)

[Longsword Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FLongsword_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

Longsword Mould found in Grymforge on an overhanging metal bridge at **(X: -626, Y: 406)**, accessed via northmost stairs. Used to craft [Adamantine Longsword](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Longsword)[bg3.wiki].

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044400957/523BF7ECBCE14C6061265E39471010B5EC21E088/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044400957/523BF7ECBCE14C6061265E39471010B5EC21E088/)

[Mace Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FMace_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

The Mace Mould, used for crafting an [Adamantine Mace](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Mace)[bg3.wiki], can be found in Grymforge near the Ancient Forge Waypoint at **(X: -614, Y: 283)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044409224/C87DC93F86F2B375B9F505B466375D25735AE7B9/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044409224/C87DC93F86F2B375B9F505B466375D25735AE7B9/)

[Scale Mail Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FScale_Mail_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

The Scale Mail Mould, used to craft [Adamantine Scale Mail](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Scale_Mail)[bg3.wiki], is located in Grymforge near the Ancient Forge Waypoint on the floor next to a standing torch at **(X: -618, Y: 264)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044437661/8D60E06728F7E0318A1FB80BF18C845457FF2013/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044437661/8D60E06728F7E0318A1FB80BF18C845457FF2013/)

[Splint Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSplint_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

The Splint Mould, used to craft [Adamantine Splint Armor](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Splint_Armour)[bg3.wiki], is found in Grymforge near the Ancient Forge Waypoint on a rock ledge overlooking lava pools at **(X: -593, Y: 311)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044449838/F23165E91D0968C107F4C91546BD19FB4C91E597/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044449838/F23165E91D0968C107F4C91546BD19FB4C91E597/)

[Scimitar Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FScimitar_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

The Scimitar Mould is found on a skeleton in Grymforge, on a ruined stairwell with two levers at **(X: -604, Y: 322)**, overlooking the rubble that has trapped Nere. The stairwell is above ground level, and jumping or spells like Misty Step are useful in reaching this location. It is used to craft the [Adamantine Scimitar](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Scimitar)[bg3.wiki].

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044521087/CCDF6EB5F42FA4AAB7D22F1523A24107F4C0F07E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044521087/CCDF6EB5F42FA4AAB7D22F1523A24107F4C0F07E/)

[Shield Mould](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FShield_Mould)[bg3.wiki]

The Shield Mould is found on a Skeleton at Grymforge, located at the very top of the stairs in the Dormitory near the room with the Merregon Legionnaire and the Hellsboars **(X: -560, Y: 410)**, and it's used to craft an [Adamantine Shield](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAdamantine_Shield)[bg3.wiki].

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044532025/ECEAE740E4549455F9B39185521CE4FEA6D399FC/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044532025/ECEAE740E4549455F9B39185521CE4FEA6D399FC/)

[Grymskull Helm](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FGrymskull_Helm)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Grym in The Adamantine Forge.

## Mountain Pass

Lady Esther

  
She is a human trader located on the Rosymorn Monastery Trail and an adventurer employed by the Society of Brilliance. Her current objective is to acquire a Githyanki egg as part of a long-term nurturing experiment. However, if you complete the conversation/quest regarding obtaining a Githyanki egg, you will no longer be able to trade with her.

## Dawnmaster's Crest

An alternative method to obtain the Blood of Lathander, though not required, is by obtaining the Dawnmaster's Crest, which involves collecting four ceremonial weapons and placing them on their respective altars.  
  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041787713/59AFF19EE29F53CFDBBE5A89F84246CE1D897C6B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167041787713/59AFF19EE29F53CFDBBE5A89F84246CE1D897C6B/)

[Ceremonial Longsword](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCeremonial_Longsword)[bg3.wiki]

The Ceremonial Longsword is found in the stained glass room; leave it where it is.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044798368/F5B052CA606E24FF41D058C00FD5AB79F2F9FE92/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044798368/F5B052CA606E24FF41D058C00FD5AB79F2F9FE92/)

[Ceremonial Warhammer](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCeremonial_Warhammer)[bg3.wiki]

This weapon can be found at Rosymorn Monastery in the Giant Eagle's nest at **(X: 80, Y: 44)**. Place the Ceremonial Warhammer on the Northeast altar to obtain Dawnmaster Seed.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044812977/3FF9700B02AC770C7E44B71D145821DC341E47C6/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044812977/3FF9700B02AC770C7E44B71D145821DC341E47C6/)

[Ceremonial Battleaxe](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCeremonial_Battleaxe)[bg3.wiki]

Found in one of the dormitories in the Rosymorn Monastery, guarded by a Guardian of Faith. Place the Ceremonial Battleaxe onto the Southwest altar for Dawnmaster Vaseid.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044824011/E24005410C6B266BC6571EA6BE4254682C8FD181/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044824011/E24005410C6B266BC6571EA6BE4254682C8FD181/)

[Ceremonial Mace](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCeremonial_Mace)[bg3.wiki]

Found on a random kobold in the Rosymorn Monastery Firewine storage room, this Ceremonial Mace should be placed onto the Southeast altar for Dawnmaster Stockhold.

## The Blood of Lathander

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044988668/97B91D89BB114B20280BB1127EF49761469F293C/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044988668/97B91D89BB114B20280BB1127EF49761469F293C/)

[The Blood of Lathander](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Blood_of_Lathander)[bg3.wiki]

In the western hallway at **(X:1330, Y:-660)**, there are two statues that need to be rotated: the one to the south requires an Athletics check to move and should be turned west, while the other should be turned east. This action opens a path in the wall with some barrels that can be jumped over or destroyed. Proceed west to reach the Secret Stairway. Use ranged attacks to destroy the Energy Source crystal on the south side of the barrier, then climb the rocks to access the next area. In the following room, there's a Dawnbreaker that emits pulses to knock the party away. Wait for breaks in the pulses to approach and disarm it (DC 14). Unfortunately, the path forward is still blocked. Move to some rocks, climb them, and follow the path to locate another energy source at **(X:1118, Y:-790)**. The final energy source is situated on the northern rocks. Avoid the Dawnbreaker's pulses and consider disarming it to easily destroy the Energy Source. Proceeding onward, the party eventually encounters the Blood of Lathander. If the Dawnmaster's Crest has been obtained, simply place it in the altar's item slot to safely acquire the Blood of Lathander. Placing the crest after taking the mace also deactivates the trap. If the Blood of Lathander is taken without the Dawnmaster's Crest, it triggers a deadly trap that jeopardizes the Monastery's destruction. The party has only four turns to either escape the Monastery or dismantle the four Lathander Solar Machines that activate the trap.

## Gale's bonuses

Bonus

How to obtain

**Level 3 Shadow Spell Slot**

Consume the Shadow Weave from the corpse of Gerringothe Thorm, Malus Thorm or Thisobald Thorm. Once and only as Gale. Cleansing the Shadow Weave grants Mystra's Blessing until Long rest instead.

**Mystra's Benevolence**

Have Gale destroy the ritual circle in Balthazar's secret room (Advantage to Concentration Checks).

## House of Healing Morgue

There are two entrances to this location: one west of the House of Healing and another east of the Grand Mausoleum waypoint through the Ominous Crevice at **(X:-150, Y:105)**.  
  

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

## Act 3 - Rivington

Ferg Drogher

  
He is the Sharran lookout watching for Shadowheart's return. If Shadowheart is in your party, he will call out, and if you initiate dialogue with him, he will walk away when the dialogue is done, never to return. Ferg has a special stock of goods, which he can be convinced to sell.

## Inside the Rivington district

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591528265/A36AF57DE8ADAAAD60081DC2BF663FCCA7231ABC/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591528265/A36AF57DE8ADAAAD60081DC2BF663FCCA7231ABC/)

[Corpsegrinder](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCorpsegrinder)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from Stone Lord Agents northwest of the Open Hand Temple **(X: -210 Y: 99)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261286420/263B55CE82012AA0467D869EF0B2BE134603361A/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261286420/263B55CE82012AA0467D869EF0B2BE134603361A/)

[Tasha's Hideous Laughter](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FTasha%2527s_Hideous_Laughter)[bg3.wiki]

Carry the Sentient Amulet from Grymforge to a casket under the Open Hand Temple in Wyrm's Crossing, completing Help the Cursed Monk. Must choose to accept the curse yourself.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263198147/D7B753A5B3D7DEAE4FC7E8150871D8D94826A6F7/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263198147/D7B753A5B3D7DEAE4FC7E8150871D8D94826A6F7/)

[Stillmaker](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FStillmaker)[bg3.wiki]

Found in a hole **(X: 67 Y: -1018)** in the area underneath the Open Hand Temple. A successful DC5 Perception check is needed to spot it. The hole is just at the top of the cragged rock and can be easy to miss. There is blood right next to the hole.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591532287/A76F15AB0C927B371B302B9BC3ECF932A0F4852E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591532287/A76F15AB0C927B371B302B9BC3ECF932A0F4852E/)

[Gleamdance Dagger](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FGleamdance_Dagger)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from the body of Farlin in one the beaches and in Sharess' Caress.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263288478/86E92B1A74F0B10E8E8654D0E16002BEF63C3132/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263288478/86E92B1A74F0B10E8E8654D0E16002BEF63C3132/)

[Amulet of the Drunkard](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAmulet_of_the_Drunkard)[bg3.wiki]

Sold by Hoots Hooligan in Sharess' Caress, found in Wyrm's Crossing.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591547325/EE444AE597A071CF215FB3B88BD1CC3677D6FA48/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591547325/EE444AE597A071CF215FB3B88BD1CC3677D6FA48/)

[Bonespike Boots](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FBonespike_Boots)[bg3.wiki]

Looted from a trapped Wooden Chest **(X: -1340 Y: -857)** in a secluded Passageway **(X: -233 Y: 84)** at the far end of the western trail leading away from the South Span Checkpoint in Rivington.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263670456/9D765CAE4DADD50F4AE97691858ACA45371D217A/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263670456/9D765CAE4DADD50F4AE97691858ACA45371D217A/)

[Ring of Truthfulnesss](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRing_of_Truthfulness)[bg3.wiki]

Dropped by Mind Flayer hiding in the Windmill Basement of the Abandoned Windmill **(X: 44 Y: -38)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591555101/E173E7D51DF6372AB3E1BB4B89D43749D900F26A/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591555101/E173E7D51DF6372AB3E1BB4B89D43749D900F26A/)

[Ring of Blink](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRing_of_Blink)[bg3.wiki]

Steal from or persuade the tressym, Tara, on the roof of the Open Hand Temple **(X: -45 Y: -5)**. Easily done with Gale in your party.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039529013/37719BD94DFE501785312751A1F8265BBEB8DAFB/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167039529013/37719BD94DFE501785312751A1F8265BBEB8DAFB/)

[The Undead Bane](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Undead_Bane)[bg3.wiki]

Carried by Ulma in the Gur Camp.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263258018/CCF384A0D1484763B5C5FB1F7D9F38EFED12FE12/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263258018/CCF384A0D1484763B5C5FB1F7D9F38EFED12FE12/)

[Absolute Confidence Amulet](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FAbsolute_Confidence_Amulet)[bg3.wiki]

Reward for initiating conversation with Naaber multiple times until the option is exhausted in Carm's Garm at Wyrm's Crossing **(X: 20 Y: 130)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263264295/81532B3C8377113F4647E8AD7B289542C8736827/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263264295/81532B3C8377113F4647E8AD7B289542C8736827/)

[Seemingly Gleaming Ring](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSeemingly_Gleaming_Ring)[bg3.wiki]

An alternate reward for talking to Naaber, if the Absolute Confidence Amulet was pickpocketed from him. Carm's Garm **(X: 20 Y: 130)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044728586/806CC21CFA2759B45D8252686031FBC8DFA3F716/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2099296167044728586/806CC21CFA2759B45D8252686031FBC8DFA3F716/)

[Seraphic Pugilist Gloves](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FSeraphic_Pugilist_Gloves)[bg3.wiki]

Can be found in the southern most locked chest at the foot of the beds at the west end of the Open Hand Temple. Location: **(X:-90, Y:-7)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263740056/A4F3435E7BB741123516D6742890FD046099DDCE/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296263740056/A4F3435E7BB741123516D6742890FD046099DDCE/)

[Tarnished Ring](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FTarnished_Ring)[bg3.wiki]

To craft it, merge the Ringless Gem (available from Ferg Drogher at **(X: 45 Y: -98)** or through pickpocketing) with the Gemless Ring (found on the Refugee Corpse at **(X: 46 Y: 66)** in northeastern Rivington).

## Orphic Hammer

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591324250/DB5D73E298DA395A19D09331EF66168FFEB6F73E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591324250/DB5D73E298DA395A19D09331EF66168FFEB6F73E/)

The Orphic Hammer can be obtained by making a deal with Raphael as part of the quest "Deal with the Devil" at Sharess' Caress. If a deal was not made with Raphael, the hammer can instead be stolen from the Archive within the House of Hope.

Jungle

Icon

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591884565/D8588EB1A4D7513D0AB9A476FE21743F6F59EA6E/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591884565/D8588EB1A4D7513D0AB9A476FE21743F6F59EA6E/)

Can be found in the Sorcerous Vault inside Sorcerous Sundries, in the vault of Elminster. It is inside an invisible chest. See Invisibility or Faerie Fire can be used to see the chest.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591890369/A01FC805DD469FBDBA4F881C85D7F74C71ECE83C/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591890369/A01FC805DD469FBDBA4F881C85D7F74C71ECE83C/)

[Robe of the Weave](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FRobe_of_the_Weave)[bg3.wiki]

Located inside an Globe of Invulnerability in Ramazith's Tower during Act Three. (Use See Invisibility to see the lever and disable the globe)

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261582489/3C8978F20AEBBFF9F6CF19FA08DDD6B0998C70E3/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261582489/3C8978F20AEBBFF9F6CF19FA08DDD6B0998C70E3/)

[Hellfire Greataxe](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHellfire_Greataxe)[bg3.wiki]

In the Illusion section of Sorcerous Sundries' Sorcerous Vault, you can find it in a chest. Once inside the vault, locate the doors with names, proceed through the 'Silverhand' door, and in the next area, destroy the door labeled 'Illusion' (attempting to enter it conventionally will not work). Beyond that door is the room with the chest located at **(X: 366 Y: 941)**.

  

Rolan

  
These items are sold at Sorcerous Sundries. If both Rolan and Lorroakan are alive, they are sold by Rolan. However, if Rolan is dead, they are sold by Lorroakan's Projection.

## Legendary Books

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591849958/07E7BF94DD16968D9DE07C0984CCB78AB7CB42B2/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591849958/07E7BF94DD16968D9DE07C0984CCB78AB7CB42B2/)

[The Annals of Karsus](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Annals_of_Karsus)[bg3.wiki]

Located in the vault of Sorcerous Sundries. The book can be found in the Karsus section at **(X: 319 Y: 1000)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591859675/188481B1E47D24023B6A3BF3EFB4C3B73BFFE171/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591859675/188481B1E47D24023B6A3BF3EFB4C3B73BFFE171/)

[The Tharchiate Codex](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Tharchiate_Codex)[bg3.wiki]

The Tharchiate Codex is stored in the Sorcerous Vault beneath Sorcerous Sundries in the Lower City. You can reach the vault in two different ways: by interacting with the Clasped Book in the bookshelf of Tolna Tome-Monger's office upstairs and going through the portal, or by using the Weave Button labeled 'Vault' on the middle level of Ramazith's Tower. Within the vault, the Codex is located in the Elminster section. To unlock the Elminster door, you must first find the lever at the end of the Silverhand section by opening doors in this order: Silverhand, Evocation, Wish.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591873111/7A5E8DA8DC1E1D637783EB26C9F5CA3E19292D19/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591873111/7A5E8DA8DC1E1D637783EB26C9F5CA3E19292D19/)

[Caution Before the Seelie](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FCaution_Before_the_Seelie)[bg3.wiki]

Located in the Sorcerous Vault beneath Sorcerous Sundries. The book can be found In a hidden library area behind an illusory wall **(X: 350 Y: 1010)**.

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591876948/DE6452C66B37F59EB3CE2BCD00AD440D20395FC0/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409591876948/DE6452C66B37F59EB3CE2BCD00AD440D20395FC0/)

[The Red Knight's Final Stratagem](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FThe_Red_Knight%2527s_Final_Stratagem)[bg3.wiki]

Located in the Sorcerous Vault beneath Sorcerous Sundries. The book can be found at **(X: 368 Y: 1057)**, behind a wall just north of the entry portal to the vault, locked behind a hidden button (requires a DC 15 Perception check to reveal).

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261298673/1E2FF1846A4FFC5CDAB9880D409978D0F1C780B9/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113934296261298673/1E2FF1846A4FFC5CDAB9880D409978D0F1C780B9/)

[Danse Macabre](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FDanse_Macabre)[bg3.wiki]

Read The Tharchiate Codex, then read the last page of the Necromancy of Thay (from Act 1) and pass a 20 Wisdom Saving Throw.

## House of Hope

Icon

Loot

How to obtain

[![](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409592629344/04DBFB1E90706CCFA48B9130A24254F003CCB60B/)](https://images.steamusercontent.com/ugc/2113933409592629344/04DBFB1E90706CCFA48B9130A24254F003CCB60B/)

[Helldusk Helmet](https://steamcommunity.com/linkfilter/?u=https%3A%2F%2Fbg3.wiki%2Fwiki%2FHelldusk_Helmet)[bg3.wiki]

Found in the House of Hope in a vault across from the entrance to the Boudoir. The player will need to pass a DC10 Wisdom check to activate the Inert Infernal Gem and then a DC20 Arcana check to use the Infernal Control Gem to open the door. The helmet will be located in the treasure pile **(X: -6482 Y: 2939)**.

The helmet will be located in the treasure pile **(X: -6482 Y: 2939)**.

Found in an Opulent Chest **(X: -32 Y: 198)** on the top floor of Wyrm's Rock Fortress.
